## Text Elements
execution of malware: - Least privilege
                      - Protection mechanism: whitelisting

exploitation of a software vulnerability: - Complete mediation
                    - Rigorous patch and change management

 ^JNACpvuf

14 ^K7NlfEtY

if an attacker ever obtains the password database, storing onlz a one waz hash prevents immediate recovery of the original passwords. They must instead expend substantial work to invert each hash, greatly raising the bar for compromise.  ^ADCKOqcM

A hash function h is a function that maps inputs of arbitrary length (x) to fixed-length outputs (h(x)), and is fast to compute while being one-way (pre-image resistant) and collision-resistant. ^fAN95R3O

- A one way function: should be able to hash but not "unhash" it.
- it should be collision resistent: two strings shouldnt give the same hash. ^MDeU9u6N

bcrypt, PBKDF2 ^WOEyFsx7

Salting of passwords is a method to add some random characters to a  password to distinct it from the real password, so that guessing the hash of common passwords in e.g. rainbow table would be impossible. ^UQKpyrjv

no because a salt is always random meaning even the same password gives different hash ^6omZ4tEW

Many iterations help against attacks because it is more costly for the attacker in terms of time spent decrypting and required computational power.  ^vnfaHaFc

the attack was successful because the company couldnt fix the vulnerabilities in time. The company was alerted of the vulnerabilities by a third party and they worked together to fix it but were not fast enough. the company took down its cloud and SaaS servers to stop the spreading of the malicious software. After the losses have ben stopped they rebuild the system from 0 with in a week ^rB7YXhr9

CC evaluations are often time-consuming and expensive.
Products may be outdated by the time certification is complete, reducing practical relevance.

Accelerated Security Certification by BSI focuses on shorter evaluation times by limiting the scope to critical security aspects and using streamlined processes.
 ^6HNWJTLO

- comprehensive design analysis
- independent vulnerability analysis (AVA_VAN)
- Independent penetration testing
- Life-cycle and development environment assessment
- testing coverage ^zfDXkvIY

The TOE Overview and description to have a clear understanding of what product is, its purpose and main security functions. Then the security problem definition (SPD), which describes assumed threats, organizational security policies and environmental assumptions. Then Security Objectives (for the TOE and the environment), which shows how the TOE adrdresses the threats and which security requirements depend on the surrounding environment. ^OtVNwo13
